<script type="text/javascript">jQuery(function ($) {
/**************************************************************/
/*AM22 Named Function to load FB box - START*/

var fb_loaded = false; //To track loading only once on a page.
function loadFaceBook()
{
    var fb_div = $("#facebookHolder");

    var top = fb_div.offset().top;

    if ( !fb_loaded && $(window).scrollTop() + $(window).height() > top )
    {
        fb_loaded = true;

        $('#facebookHolder').append('<div id="fb-root"></div>');
        $('#facebookHolder').append('<div class="fb-page" data-href="https://www.facebook.com/grao.ed/" data-tabs="timeline" data-small-header="true" data-adapt-container-width="true" data-hide-cover="false" data-show-facepile="true"><blockquote cite="https://www.facebook.com/grao.ed/" class="fb-xfbml-parse-ignore"><a href="https://www.facebook.com/grao.ed/"><?= Graó Educació ?></a></blockquote></div>');

        (function(d, s, id) {
          var js, fjs = d.getElementsByTagName(s)[0];
          if (d.getElementById(id)) return;
          js = d.createElement(s); js.id = id;
          js.src = "//connect.facebook.net/ca_ES/sdk.js#xfbml=1&version=v2.9";
          fjs.parentNode.insertBefore(js, fjs);
        }(document, 'script', 'facebook-jssdk'));

        $('#twitterHolder').append('<a class="twitter-timeline" data-height="500" href="https://twitter.com/EditorialGrao">Tweets by EditorialGrao</a>');
        jQuery.getScript('//platform.twitter.com/widgets.js');
     }
}

/*AM22 Named Function to load FB - END*/
/*AM22 Facebook Lazy load - START*/

$(function () {
   var fb_div = $("#facebookHolder");
   if (fb_div.size() > 0) {
     $(window).scroll(loadFaceBook);
   }
});
/*AM22 Facebook lazy load - END*/
